Thus, a development-based system aims to clarify the rights, duties, and responsibilities of teaching, teaching, teaching specialists, teaching directorate. In addition, we see that the health of contract teachers has been granted the right to be appointed where their safety is in distress. However, we also know that teachers have expectations for appointments related to their spouse situation. In addition, there is a revolutionary study of teaching career steps in the context of the law. The teacher's profession is divided into three career steps, including a teacher, an expert teacher, and a head teacher, after the candidate teaching period. If our teachers participate in an expert teacher's training programme, which is not less than a hundred and eighty hours into vocational development, they will be subject to an expert teacher's test, and our teachers who have received 70 points will be eligible to receive an expert teacher's certificate. However, our teachers who are entitled to one level and to become attending teachers will receive an additional income of over a thousand lira on their salary. Our teachers who have a master's degree will be exempt from their expert teaching exams. Again, teachers who have a ten-year course in expert teaching, with no less than two hundred and forty hours, will receive 70 points in the written examination for vocational development will be entitled to a master's certificate, thus earning more than 2,000 lira in addition to their salary. Our PhD teachers will also be exempted from this examination. Thus, the fact that our teachers are being rewarded both for their Master's and Ph.D. is also important for their career. And what we can see from here is that in the coming years, there will also be a serious increase in grad school and PhD rates. However, on 26/10/2018 as the National Movement Party, we offered a law proposal for the Grand National Assembly of Turkey on 3600 additional indicators. The fact that this proposal comes out in the professional code is also very rewarding for us. We see that the 3600 additional indicators contribute both to our teachers' pension and their salary. There's a very serious improvement in that sense. As a matter of fact, it is clear that this offer has an additional cost to the budget between 16 and 20 billion.
